vpc_repair_ruler = {
 if = { limit = { is_independent_ruler = yes NOT = { voltara_is_ruins_placeholder = yes }
  OR = { culture = culture:voltara_culture_0 culture = culture:voltara_culture_44 }
  OR = { exists = capital_county exists = location.county } }
  if = { limit = { exists = capital_county capital_county = { NOT = { culture = culture:voltara_culture_0 } NOT = { culture = culture:voltara_culture_44 } } }
   set_culture = capital_county.culture
  } else_if = { limit = { exists = capital_county capital_county = { has_variable = vpc_local_culture } }
   set_culture = capital_county.var:vpc_local_culture
  } else_if = { limit = { exists = location.county location.county = { NOT = { culture = culture:voltara_culture_0 } NOT = { culture = culture:voltara_culture_44 } } }
   set_culture = location.county.culture
  } else_if = { limit = { exists = location.county location.county = { has_variable = vpc_local_culture } }
   set_culture = location.county.var:vpc_local_culture
  }
 }
}
vpc_repair_world = {
 every_county = { limit = { culture = culture:voltara_culture_0 has_variable = vpc_local_culture }
  set_county_culture = var:vpc_local_culture
 }
 every_independent_ruler = { vpc_repair_ruler = yes }
}
